============================================================
0. test config
------------------------------------------------------------
userId        = demo-1783653182355
recordId      = record-1783653182346
modelFileName = D:\works\factory-planner\call-fs-mcp\snowy-web-app\target\test-classes\step-by-step.fs
showAnimation = false
maxPollSeconds= 10
unloadAtEnd  = true
MQTTX subscribe topic: factory/sim/#
Note: showAnimation=false usually finishes quickly, so pause/resume may be skipped.

{
	"jsonrpc":"2.0",
	"id":"2",
	"result":{
		"tools":[
			{

				"inputSchema":{

						},

						"modelId"

				"name":"updateRemoteSimulationParameterTable",

							"type":"string",

						},

						},

							"type":"string",
							"description":"JSON array of rows. Each row is an array matching the table columns, or an object keyed by column name."

						"modelId",

						"rowsJson"

				}
			},

						},

							"type":"string",

						},

						},

			{

							"description":"Model file name or absolute path on the simulation engine server."

						"recordId",
						"modelFileName"
					]

			},
			{
				"name":"getRemoteSimulationResultSnapshot",

					"type":"object",

						},
